## Symmetric versus downside conditional variance: actually registered

The [new actual packet](evidence/conditional_variance_growth_20261029_registration_v1.json)
contains two fixed configurations: `symmetric_garch_growth_bil_v1`
and `downside_gjr_growth_bil_v1`. The question is whether an
additional negative-squared-return response improves conditional
risk allocation relative to symmetric variance persistence.
Neither a new semiconductor cap nor a retrospectively selected
2018 filter is introduced.

For each eligible original month end, 253 known adjusted closes
produce 252 log returns of a fixed stock risk basket: half SOXX
and one eighth in each of MTUM, VLUE, QUAL and USMV. Each
daily basket gross return is the weighted sum of constituent
gross returns, followed by the log transform. This describes
a **synthetic constant-mix gross risk proxy**, not the drifted
holdings of a funded portfolio, a traded zero-cost strategy
or a replacement benchmark. The fits do not use SPY, QQQ,
VIX or VXN values.

Both zero-mean Gaussian quasi-likelihood models initialize
variance at the observed training second moment. In normalized
units this is one. The symmetric recurrence has an intercept,
lagged squared return and lagged conditional variance; GJR
adds a nonnegative coefficient on a negative lagged squared
return. The parameters are separately estimated, not held
equal across the two models. One deterministic initialization
per fit preserves initial persistence 0.95 and intercept0.05
in both models. A positive-slack softmax parameterization
keeps persistence strictly below one; normalized intercept
and logit bounds are explicit in the
[frozen policy](config/conditional-variance-growth-v1.json).

The first forecast uses the last observed shock. Further
expected variances use persistence equal to alpha plus beta
plus half gamma. The half-gamma term requires **symmetric,
unit-second-moment innovations**; it is not an empirical
finding about the stock basket. All21 forecast variances
enter the mean, without simulation or division by an
almost-zero one-minus-persistence denominator.
The [official model documentation](https://arch.readthedocs.io/en/latest/univariate/univariate_volatility_modeling.html)
and [forecasting reference](https://arch.readthedocs.io/en/latest/univariate/forecasting.html)
support the standard recurrences and analytic forecasting
principle. Their constant-mean SP500 examples, data and
significance results are not used. This bounded zero-mean
SciPy implementation is not claimed to reproduce that package
or an original paper exactly; no package was installed.

The fixed allocation is:

```text
stock = 0.98 * min(1, pooled_252_day_second_moment / mean_next_21_variance)
SOXX = stock / 2
each of MTUM, VLUE, QUAL, USMV = stock / 8
BIL = 0.98 - stock
target idle cash = 0.02
```

There is no absolute volatility target and no inherited14.7%
HMM stock floor. Thus a comparison against the old HMM
changes the risk proxy, inference and budget: it is a whole
package comparison, not isolated GARCH causal alpha.
Within the matched pair, the extra downside-response term
and its estimated coefficient distinguish the model families.
Neither model's variance forecast guarantees the15% drawdown
gate. Actual trading remains monthly band-triggered, with
the original delays, costs, fractional cash funding and RF.
BIL may be zero; actual tickets and turnover must be measured,
not assumed from a fixed asset count.

Actual lifecycle, all on **2026-10-29 UTC**:

|---|---|
| GARCH registration322 | 12:25:39.373023 |
| GJR registration323 | 12:25:39.501898 |
| First real model-fit attempt | 12:25:39.901900 |
| Last of244 fit receipts | 12:26:08.974125 |

| Completed registration packet | 12:26:15.077738 |

Every one of the122 eligible dates has a separate start and
completion receipt for each model. The first nine original

anchors and all four style families are covered. Five prefix
checks per model compare exact observed inputs and reconstruct
the saved fit without another optimization.

| Model | Completed fits | Optimizer iteration range | Fits near a numerical coordinate bound | Maximum independent target difference | Maximum relative variance difference |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| GARCH | 122 | 10--55 | 8 | 6.67e-15 | 1.54e-14 |
| GJR | 122 | 14--61 | 22 | 5.00e-15 | 1.54e-14 |

rounded upward. Bound counts refer to fits with at
least one near-bound coordinate, not the number of distinct
constraints or proof of identified parameters. Every fit
uses the single registered initialization. Local numerical
convergence is not a global optimum, forecast calibration,
statistical significance or performance evidence.

|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|

The ten-year gains come with worse drawdown and implementation

fall by USD1 while proportional costs rise by USD8.520458.
Equal rebalance-count totals would not establish equal dates
or traded notional, and no such equivalence is assumed.

0.197288 percentage points. The original stress scenario
changes both cost and execution delay, and the original
holding-gap feedback can change the resulting path.
This is not evidence that higher fees improve returns,
nor permission to replace a case or execution clock.

### Real Interface and one capital account

`StrategyExpert.observe(as_of, lookback)` is the small
Interface at the archived recommendation/observation Seam.
The dense and monthly Adapters hide different missing-data
and carry semantics while preserving actual clocks.
Missing dense rows or scheduled month ends after warmup
are errors, not safe-state defaults. An expert can have
an available recommendation but an immature score.

The common market reuses the already admitted14-asset
panel, preserving every original12-asset price cell and RF.
For a12-asset expert, IJR/PKW are structural zero exposures,
not imputed prices or missing recommendations. Shadow
observations reuse the exact existing **ten-year-base net
account records**, with2,512 dated returns. No old entry
point, GARCH fit, LP solution or expert account was rerun.

Each shadow book began at its own historical USD10,000
reference scale and subsequently drifted. Its effective
fixed-fee drag is not necessarily the cost of a fractional
expert allocation or a fresh five-year master account.
No shadow wealth is added to the main account, and no
averaged CAGR, drawdown or gross-wealth guarantee is used.
The main target is a convex mixture of complete expert
recommendations, netted at the actual underlying assets,
with98% invested and2% target idle cash.

### First fixed pair

The [frozen policy](config/strategy-expert-meta-v1.json)
contains an equal-weight control and one rolling rule.
These numbers are research design choices, not user
requirements or the result of a parameter sweep.

At each completed month end, the rolling rule takes126
common matured net shadow observations: the older84 train
pair risk weights and the later42 validate the candidate
pairs. Only distinct-mechanism pairs are considered.
Normalized inverse training standard deviations equalize
the two marginal variance contributions, including their
covariance. Pair portfolio risk, rather than a standalone
return ranking, enters both train and validation scores.

Selection uses the lower of the two annualized net-excess
risk-adjusted scores after a fixed incremental meta-switch
cost estimate. Score keys use12 decimal places, with
lexical expert-ID ties and unrounded values retained.
Negative scores remain negative; there is no retrospectively
chosen cash threshold. Zero-risk pairs cannot become
infinite-Sharpe winners.

The cost proxy compares new expert weights with the previous
expert weights applied to the **same current recommendations**.
It estimates incremental asset turnover at5bps plus USD1
per changed asset, referenced to USD10,000 and spread over
21 sessions. This is a conservative selection proxy, not an
exact main-account fee or an additional charge to capital.
The main book independently pays its actual scenario costs.

Before the126-observation history matures, the rolling rule
uses explicitly **unscored equal weights**. Those initial
gains, losses and fees remain in every applicable formal
account; no date is removed or declared safe. Missing
post-start observations still fail. The seven unscored
decisions run from2016-09-30 through2017-03-31; the first
scored decision is **2017-04-28**. There are114 scored
decisions and zero degenerate-pair fallback decisions in
this actual preparation.

Both main strategies use the original **monthly2.5pp
actual-holdings feedback**, fresh USD10,000 per formal
path,1/2-session delays,5/20bps,USD1 tickets, RF and initial
capital peak. Some source experts' native shadow accounts
use daily drift feedback. Monthly main execution therefore
does not exactly reproduce their native wealth; the final
net account, not the shadow scoring proxy, must meet the gates.

commitment,26.499396% joint-tail and33.245385% GARCH.
Distinct mechanisms did not guarantee low realized
dependence: the selected pair's stored training correlation
has median0.824529 and mean0.663378, with range
-0.284590 to0.999999. The selected pair has the highest
available stored correlation65 times;54 selections have
correlation above0.9. These counts are descriptive, **not
new acceptance gates or reasons to retroactively exclude
an expert**.

The selected pair's training variance-contribution shares
are each one half, to numerical precision. That balances
two components *within the measured training pair*; it
sets neither an absolute master-portfolio variance ceiling
nor a bound on future drawdown or stock exposure.
There are43 negative selected scores: the frozen rule
ranks relative alternatives without a fitted cash cutoff.
None of these observations changes its parameters.

the five-year comparisons. The same cost proxy
still does not guarantee low actual trading cost.

isolated expert marginal alpha, a claim of independent
risk sources or a fee-free counterfactual. Exposed
pool-selection bias and the difference between
native shadow wealth and fresh master capital remain.
The expert and both failed combinations stay archived
and available for admissible future research.

## Actual-main wealth protection after complete expert composition

The [new policy](config/expert-master-protection-v1.json)
tests whether the original ratchet law applied to the
combined main account's own net wealth can change the
failure observed when protection belonged only to an
expert's shadow state. This is a new application at the
expert-composition layer, **not** a new economic factor,
first discovery of CPPI, new floor or fitted multiplier.
The earlier growth/semiconductor protection failures
and all four-expert outcomes remain intact.

Two [actual registrations](evidence/expert_master_protection_20261029_registration_v1.json)
occurred at **2026-10-29T22:20:33.261167Z** and
**22:20:33.383687Z**:
`expert_pool_four_equal_master_protection_v1` and
`expert_pool_four_rolling_master_protection_v1`.
The code/input freeze intent SHA256 is
`3ab3b8bbe0c8d5fa1d570019307721ab5d6505c806ba27a204b05eacc7568aed`;
the registered specification SHA256 is
`c4ed10476bc126d4e65ac46e8fbf182659accbbc7c1a11d3e69af9084558a6b9`.

The unchanged four experts,126/84/42 assessment, two-expert
risk weights, ranking and cost proxy are not regenerated.
The exact previously proved equal/rolling nominal target
matrices, SHA256`5635ac90...` and`80f62c3b...`, are reused.
That is source reuse, not a claim that new combined targets
or scores were generated. No source download, new shadow
book or original account replay is required.

Each formal main path starts with its own USD10,000,
zero units and USD10,000 initial peak. After an actual
close, including actual execution costs, its own closing
NAV updates its own peak. The unchanged original
`semiconductor_portfolio_protection.protected_target`
law scales **all non-BIL nominal assets**, including
gold or longer Treasury funds, by the clipped cushion
relative to85% of that main peak and15% of current NAV.
Every removed target weight moves to actual BIL,
preserving existing nominal BIL, the98% ETF budget,
long-only1x and target2% idle cash. An expert's NAV,
peak, native five-point band or funded wealth is never
imported into main capital.

Unlike the old monthly master, this policy inspects
actual main protection **daily** at completed closes.
It retains the original2.5-percentage-point actual
holdings band and its numerical tolerance, original
5/20bps plus USD1 model tickets,1/2-session delayed
opens, fractional funding, original RF and every formal
10y/5y return date. Month ends do not force below-band
orders. There is only one pending request, never
cancelled or overwritten. A nominal month-end update
while pending remains the latest known intention;
the next eligible close evaluates it using then-actual
main wealth. Final unfilled requests are retained
without terminal liquidation.

### Frozen transition proof is not a future issued-target matrix

Actual main-state-dependent issued targets cannot be
precomputed without already running their funded path.
The [new execution Adapter](src/us_quant/expert_master_protection_v1.py)
therefore uses the existing small funded-execution
Interface but separates its fixed nominal input from
the targets it issues with actual path state.
The public registration's `target_proof.targets`
field explicitly denotes only the old nominal source.

### A mathematical transfer, not renamed asset sleeves

The project already has a uniform simplex learner over
core/SOXX/GLD gross price relatives. Its mathematical
moment-integration idea is prior art, not a new discovery.
Here the inputs are four **complete policy** recommendations
and preserved NET shadow accounts from their existing
technical admissions. Existing dense and monthly Adapters
serve the small `observe` Interface. No old expert source,
fit, target generator, admission entry point or funded
account is rerun.

The uniform Dirichlet(1,1,1,1) prior covers constant
fractions of those complete net-return streams. Each
constant fraction vector scores the product of its
mixed full-month relatives; a normalized tetrahedral
moment recursion integrates the mean fractions.
All four fractions remain positive. No historical
best weight, sampled winner, weight grid, fitted prior,
temperature or forgetting parameter is selected.
The internal monomial states are algebra, **not additional
economic research configurations**.

This objective concerns complete-mixture growth and
diversification, not just maximum standalone CAGR.
Known net-excess daily covariance and marginal mixture
risk are retained over complete learning intervals.
Degenerate risk states are explicit, not successful
safe defaults or an absolute drawdown cap.

### Expanding training and next-month prequential validation

The genuine October5-2016 recommendation initializes
the main account. October31 is only the first completed
month-end anchor. The initial October6--31 partial
shadow month is not falsely treated as a full learning
interval. The first complete interval ends
**2016-11-30**. The two initial decisions use an explicitly
unscored uniform prior; **every formal main return day,
including that initial partial month, remains**.

At each subsequent month end, validate the previous
decision's fractions against the newly matured full
month **before** incorporating that month into training.
Then update the expanding learner for the next period.
There is no chosen finite lookback or five-year learner
reset. The fresh five-year main account still starts
with its own USD10,000 and peak; its observable learner
history may already contain the earlier completed months.

The archived Adapter's requested2,512-observation-window
filled flag is not this learner's score-maturity rule.
All actually available net-return/RF prefixes must be
complete; one genuine full interval makes this specified
assessment mature. No earlier missing returns are padded
to satisfy a nominal count.

These are chronological **shadow-score** validation
records, not new independent holdout data or realized
main-account returns. The pool and question follow213
already exposed completions. Actual policy registration,
expert admission and counterfactual historical availability
remain distinct.

Source net returns already contain each shadow book's
own fees, wealth, trade clocks and internal policy.

### Exact packed computation with separately measured process memory

Blindly extending the old dense array would allocate120to
the fourth power float64cells,**1,658,880,000bytes**.
The [new numerical Module](src/us_quant/expert_five_net_wealth_mixture_v1.py)
instead stores only valid nonnegative five-coordinate
degree compositions. At119intervals there are9,078,630states:
one packed coefficient array is72,629,040bytes and the largest
simultaneous old/new coefficient buffers total142,896,160bytes.
Updates stream32,768states at a time;no dense four-dimensional
120-sided tensor is allocated.

These are coefficient-array bounds,**not total processRAM**.
The synthetic full119interval test measured peak resident memory
**1,010,503,680bytes** on macOS. Actual source preparation
measured**856,854,528bytes**. The different complete processes
include their Python/package/index/temporary overhead;neither is
represented by only the72.6MBprincipal coefficient array.
Numerical states are not separately tested economic strategies.

The independent oracle uses a different representation:
polynomial-exact four-dimensional stick-breaking Gaussian-Jacobi
integration,streamed in4,096-node chunks rather than constructing
the entire quadrature mesh. Its maximum order61corresponds to
13,845,841numerical points,not a candidate-weight search.
Every actual prefix/allocation is checked. Maximum weight,
target and prequential-score differences are respectively
`5.134781488891349e-15`,
`4.551914400963142e-15`and
`7.549516567451064e-15`.

2016-10-31,2016-11-30,2020-01-31and2023-05-31.

publication/execution checks plus one full119interval sparse
stress check. The stress check reaches the exact maximum
packed state,retains uniform fifths for identical streams and
rejects an interval beyond the frozen bound. No complete source
admission,expert fit,recommendation,account or historical

control. Equal and rolling five-policy books are same-pool
architecture comparators,not isolated expert-alpha attribution.

## Independent IBKR price-history source boundary

The prepared Gateway is authorized only for existing-entitlement market data,
not account synchronization or orders. `ib_async` 2.1.0's high-level
`IB.connectAsync(readonly=True)` still requests positions; the existing paper
handshake also reads account identities. Neither path is used here.

`us_quant.ibkr_market_history.MarketHistory` is a separate, single-use,
loopback-only adapter. Its transport permits only the protocol handshake,
start-API message 71, instrument identification 9, and bounded historical-price
request 20. The initial source probe is restricted to SPY/QQQ, USD/SMART, daily
three-month `TRADES` or `ADJUSTED_LAST`, regular hours and no live subscription.
It cannot request snapshots, accounts, positions, executions, orders or order
cancellation. Every actual send rechecks the accepted SSD runtime and deadline.

Unsolicited managed-account frames are discarded before SDK decoding, logging
or source capture. Readiness requires the actual negotiated protocol and
request-ID acknowledgement, not dummy account identifiers or a fabricated SDK
account-ready flag. It proves only the market-request handshake, not data
entitlement or a paper/live account type. Provider errors retain numeric codes,
not potentially identifying text, and fail the single attempt without retries.
Malformed messages, source-persistence failures, disconnects and timeouts cannot
be converted into empty successful datasets.

The isolated synthetic-transport suite has 68 passing cases, including
fragmented/coalesced private startup frames, forbidden outgoing operations,
timeouts, deadline enforcement and output failures. This offline evidence is
not proof that any real Gateway request or historical-data admission succeeded.
Each actual probe still needs its own source-bound receipts and permitted raw
responses, retained privately on SSD.

[IBKR's historical-bar documentation](https://interactivebrokers.github.io/tws-api/historical_bars.html)
distinguishes split-adjusted `TRADES` from split-and-dividend-adjusted
`ADJUSTED_LAST`. A current-time request can include an unfinished daily bar;
calendar, completed-session cutoff, actual coverage and adjustment behavior
must be checked separately before admission. Do not silently substitute
unadjusted prices, double-count dividends, replace registered inputs, infer
ten-year coverage from a short probe, or backfill missed forward observations.

The one actual probe stopped on unknown status 2157 before a historical
request; no history was admitted. A bounded documentation review obtained
the complete warning-code section of the
[official reference](https://interactivebrokers.github.io/tws-api/message_codes.html):
it includes 2158 but not 2157. A newer official entry failed at the transport
layer, without HTTP content. Neither observation proves a subscription denial,
document nonexistence or the absence of a definition in all official material.
The existing unknown-status shutdown remains; no whitelist change or
automatic reconnection followed.
